                                 the right. As he watched, this "Star" broke into 2 parts. He

                                 the group watched the object. ,Wit~ binoculars he could see
                                 4 parts instead of 2 and at one time 5 parts. They wore all as
                     •
                                 bright as Venus at its brightest. The objects (4) spread within
                                                    '.    I
                                 the angle of the binoculars : .There was a very slight movement
                                 to the NW !rom N. No' sound. Th.ese were observed for 10 mins. to
